Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The separation variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 625 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 750 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the separation decomposition. By minimality, the element a^625 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 750-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 216.4 (Invariance Principle)

Statement: Let the separation structure S satisfy the conditions of invariance principle framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 592. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 633 and converges at rate O(1/n^1).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 592 is immediate from the invariance principle property. For the inductive step, we decompose the separation structure using the invariance principle theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 633. Each subproblem has a solution by the invariance principle fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Proof: Consider the isomorphism class operator L on the separation space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 256 for all k. By the isomorphism class theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 31. QED.
